Integrated Google Takeout + Phone Evidence View

Dark-mode jury-friendly view: Google activity records, recovered screenshot/message timing, and copied local media in the same cards.

Google Photos Backup Significance

Plain-language point: the screenshots were not only found in the iPhone message export. Google Photos metadata in the Takeout account shows iOS-origin image rows at the same times as many screenshot captures, often 0-1 seconds apart, followed by text-message transmission seconds later.

If this Takeout is from keeganhurd@gmail.com, this supports the inference that the iPhone used to capture the screenshots was configured/logged in to back up photos to that Google Photos account. It does not, standing alone, identify the physical user.
